New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe guide: Acorn Plains Star Coins

How to find every Star Coin in Acorn Plains

By Kelly Hudson Sundberg  Feb 18, 2019, 8:58am EST

e Acorn Plains Star Coins guide

Part of New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe Star Coins guide

Acorn Plains in New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe is a map filled with rolling hills (literally, they roll you backward like a treadmill), moving rocks, and plenty of classic Mario enemies to stomp or avoid. Check the spot to the left of Toad’s House for a free Super Acorn to add to your items.

ACORN PLAINS 1 – ACORN PLAINS WAY

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: This one is in plain sight, right after you get your first Super Acorn. A trail of coins will appear leading you to the first Star Coin.

FEATURED VIDEOS FROM POLYGON

Awful Squad Reunion

00:00

140:35

Awful Squad Reunion

Star Coin 2: As soon as you come back to earth after grabbing that first Star Coin, you’ll see a pipe. Travel down it to find a room filled with coins, including the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: After the red coin challenge, you’ll come to a tall platform and see a trail of coins reaching out of the sky just beyond it. Jump up to the platform and use a glide to follow the coins. At the right moment, hit R to boost yourself onto a platform. There’s a pipe here that leads you to a bonus area with many coins and your final Star Coin.

ACORN PLAINS 2 – TILTED TUNNEL

 GRID VIEW

1 of 6

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: This one is right on your path. When you slide down a long slope full of Goombas and coins, there’s a jump at the end that will take you sailing right through the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: A little bit after the checkpoint, you’ll encounter a red Koopa Troopa — don’t hit it with a fireball! Knock it out, and send the shell to the right side of the screen. It’ll hit a brick near the bottom, which will grow into a vine. Climb up the vine, and you’ll see a pipe. Jump in the pipe, and it will turn into a cannon and shoot you directly through a bunch of coins and the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: Just after you finish the red coin challenge, you’ll see a pipe blocked off by a crystal — but there’s also a sign with a down arrow. Ground pound the floor next to the sign to move the crystals, access the pipe, and collect the final Star Coin of the level.

ACORN PLAINS TOWER – CRUSHING COGS TOWER

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: After you climb the first cog and jump onto the ledge above it, look up to see the first Star Coin in a shaft above you — it’ll be alternately hidden and revealed with a rising stone block. When it’s visible, wall jump up the shaft to reach it.

Star Coin 2: The second Star Coin is below and to the left of the small cog that holds this level’s red coin challenge. You can reach it with a spin jump off the cog or using a hidden coin brick to get across. (It’s at the center of the spinning wheel of coins.)

Star Coin 3: After the spot where you have to squeeze between three fast-moving cogs, jump up and to the right to find a small ledge. From there, jump up and right again, and you’ll find yourself in a hidden tunnel that leads to the final Star Coin. It’s above you, but when you jump down you’ll find a pipe that will shoot you up to get it — and bypass some cogs.

ACORN PLAINS 3 – YOSHI HILL

 GRID VIEW

1 of 6

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: You’ll see the first Star Coin right after you hit the first coin block that contains Yoshi’s egg. It’s too high for you to reach it alone, so use Yoshi’s flutter jump to snag it.

Star Coin 2: You’ll see what looks like two bricks embedded in the ground. Ground pound them to break through to a hidden tunnel that leads to a pipe. Take the pipe to a secret room where you’ll see the Star Coin hovering over a bunch of Piranha Plants — hopefully you’ve still got Yoshi to flutter over them.

Star Coin 3: After the checkpoint, you’ll come to a series of ledges where one, then two, then three Monty Moles attack you. Jump down the gap to the right of the tallest ledge — it looks like certain death, but it’s actually a pipe. Once again, you’ll need Yoshi’s flutter jump to reach the Star Coin, so hopefully the Monty Moles didn’t scare him off.

ACORN PLAINS 4 – MUSHROOM HEIGHTS

 GRID VIEW

Star Coin 1: Collect the Balloon Baby Yoshi you’ll find on the map before this level because you’ll need it for this Star Coin. You’ll see a line of coins leading up to a high blue platform — inflate Balloon Baby Yoshi and fly up to collect the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: You’ll need the Balloon Baby Yoshi for this Star Coin as well. You’ll see it after the checkpoint, by another blue platform — but this time it’s hanging underneath. Fly low and claim it.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin is nestled below a series of swinging platforms, guarded by two Giant Piranha Plants. You’ll need a fire flower to slay the plants, and Balloon Baby Yoshi, while not essential, will make collecting the Star Coin a snap.

ACORN PLAINS 5 – RISE OF THE PIRANHA PLANTS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is near the beginning of the level, hanging above a Giant Piranha Plant. Wait until the platform holding the plant descends enough so you can jump over it and claim your Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: Fireballs are essential for this Star Coin. You’ll see a Giant Piranha Plant hanging upside-down guarding a pipe. Kill it and enter the pipe — it will take you to a secret room where three more Giant Piranha Plants guard the Star Coin. Slay them all and collect your prize.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin of the level is easily visible, hanging above a Hammer Bro. near the end of the level.

ACORN PLAINS CASTLE – LEMMY’S SWINGBACK CASTLE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: You’ll see the first Star Coin between two swinging platforms near the beginning of the level. Use a running leap or your Super Acorn glide to reach it.

Star Coin 2: Right after the checkpoint, you’ll see this Star Coin below some platforms. You’ll need a Super Acorn — use your glide to snag it.

Star Coin 3: After the red coin challenge, you’ll step onto a long series of swinging platforms with plenty of Podobos to avoid. Keep your eyes peeled for a pipe sticking out from the lava. Take it to a secret room with the final Star Coin hovering above the lava — unless you’re a truly talented platformer, you’ll need your Super Acorn glide to capture it.

ACORN PLAINS A – BLOOPER’S SECRET LAIR

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: This Star Coin is pretty easy to see — it’s at the top of the screen, between two green pipes blowing bubbles downward. Wait for the yellow pipe below them to blow bubbles upward, then swim up to claim your prize.

Star Coin 2: This Star Coin is also easy to see, but harder to collect. It’s under a ledge and seemingly blocked off. Swim under the red pipe to the left of the coin (you’ll go off the screen) to snag it.

Star Coin 3: After you take a pipe to the second stage of the level, keep your eyes on the left side of the screen. As you work your way up, you’ll eventually see three coins outside the entrance of a pipe. Take that pipe to find your final Star Coin.

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe guide: Layer-Cake Desert Star Coins

How to find every Star Coin in Layer-Cake Desert

By Kelly Hudson Sundberg  Feb 18, 2019, 8:58am EST

Share this story

Share this on Facebook (opens in new window)

Share this on Twitter (opens in new window)

SHARE

All sharing options

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe guide: Layer-Cake Desert Star Coins

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Part of New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe Star Coins guide

Watch out for quicksand! New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe’s Layer-Cake Desert is filled with it, plus the enemies you’ve come to expect from Mario desert levels: Pokeys, Fire Snakes, Spikes, and more. On part of the map, you can fight Goombas and Fire Bros. plus pick up a free Super Acorn.

LAYER-CAKE DESERT 1 — STONE-EYE ZONE STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is pretty straightforward — you’ll see it floating above a giant Stone-Eye platform. It will tip over when you jump on it, though, so be ready to jump up to collect the Star Coin, or use your Super Acorn glide jump.

FEATURED VIDEOS FROM POLYGON

The Great Game: The Making of Spycraft Teaser Trailer

The never-before-told story of how a former KGB Major General and an ex-CIA director worked together… on a video game.

Star Coin 2: You’ll need a Super Acorn for the next Star Coin. Just after the checkpoint, you’ll see an arrow pointing forward. You have to get on top of the palm tree next to this sign — to do this, get on the moving Stone-Eye to the left and use your glide jump when the Stone-Eye is at its highest point. When you’re on the palm tree, you can enter a pipe, which will take you to a room with plenty of coins (once you press the nearby P Switch) and the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin is easily visible on your path — it’s in the middle of a sand spout. To collect it, you can time the sand’s movement or just use your Super Acorn glide jump.

LAYER-CAKE DESERT 2 — PERILOUS POKEY CAVE STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: Right after you see the first Pokey on the ceiling, you’ll come to an area with sand spouts and floating blocks. Get on top of the moving question block to reach the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: Just after the first Star Coin, you’ll see two ceiling Pokeys with a sand spout between them. At the top of the sand spout, jump up to reveal a hidden coin block. After you jump on the coin block, you can jump through the gap in the ceiling and run through a line of coins, with the Star Coin at the end.

Star Coin 3: It’s a good idea to have Yoshi for this part — his egg is right before you enter the dark room with the bats. In the dark room, look for a green pipe on the ceiling and use Yoshi’s flutter jump to reach the pipe. You’ll enter a room with several Pokeys — use Yoshi to jump on their heads and reach the final Star Coin.

LAYER-CAKE DESERT 3 — FIRE SNAKE CAVERN STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: At the beginning of the cave will be a few tilting platforms. Head to the top platform and jump above the ceiling (use a Super Acorn glide jump to make it easier). You’ll find a passage leading to the first Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: Having Glowing Baby Yoshi will help for this one. Just after the checkpoint will be some long tilting platforms with bricks in the middle. Ground pound the brick on the lower platform to reach a pipe that leads to a secret room. Use Glowing Baby Yoshi to eat the fire snakes, then activate the P Switch to clear away the bricks blocking your path to the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: Near the end of the level, you’ll find two Fire Bros. on tilting platforms. Above the first one is a pipe that you’ll take to reach the final Star Coin.

LAYER-CAKE DESERT TOWER — STONESLIDE TOWER STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: You’ll see this Star Coin as soon as you turn the first crank to lower the block. Jump up to grab it before it slides out of view.

Star Coin 2: You’ll see a sign with an up arrow on the left side of the screen. On the right side is a crank you’ll turn to move two small square blocks and one rectangular block. You’ll need to get around that long block, so hurry and use your wall jump to get past it on the left side before it slams back into place. Once you’re above the block, reaching the Star Coin is easy.

Star Coin 3: In the room after the checkpoint, you’ll eventually see a shallow quicksand area on the left side of the screen. At the very left edge of this area, jump up to find a hidden pipe. You’ll be taken to a room where Grrrolls fall from the ceiling. It may take a few tries to get the timing right, but you can spin the crank to lead them to break the walls guarding the final Star Coin.

LAYER-CAKE DESERT 4 — SPIKE’S SPROUTING SAND STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is easy to see, guarded by two Spikes. Defeat them and use the coin blocks they were standing on to jump up to your Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: The first time you encounter a Spike on top of a sand spout, take him out and then jump to reveal a hidden block with a vine on top of the spout. Jump to the vine (it’ll take some trying, but you can do it), and climb it to reach the second Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: After the checkpoint, look for a signpost with an arrow going diagonally up and to the right. Below this sign is a hidden pipe — enter it, claim the Mini Mushroom, and run up the wall to the mini pipe. Then, get ready to run. Sprint through the level before the sand spouts lift you up, and grab the Star Coin at the end.

LAYER-CAKE DESERT 5 — DRY DESERT MUSHROOMS STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is right in your path — you just have to drop down to a low platform and avoid the boulders that the Spikes above throw.

Star Coin 2: Just after the checkpoint, you’ll see the second Star Coin between two Spikes on an expanding and contracting pink platform. Time your jumps carefully to collect it.

Star Coin 3: Look for a green pipe at the bottom of the screen — it leads to a secret room with a POW Block you can toss. Make sure you time your throw so the coins and Star Coin come down while the platforms are expanded.

LAYER-CAKE DESERT 6 — BLOOMING LAKITUS STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is easy to see, above some bricks with a few Piranha Plants, a red Koopa Troopa, and of course the Lakitu.

Star Coin 2: After the first giant Piranha Plant of the level, you’ll see a high platform that you can use to jump on the Lakitu. After you take it out, you can use its cloud to move up to a pipe, which will take you to a room with the second Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: The third Star Coin is near the end of the level, when you cross a bunch of rolling hills with Piranha Plants. It’s hiding behind a giant Pirahna Plant. You may have to sacrifice your power-ups to get it, or you can throw a Koopa Troopa shell at it.

LAYER-CAKE DESERT CASTLE — MORTON’S COMPACTOR CASTLE STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: You’ll see the first Star Coin above some short gaps in the floor. Get up speed and run across without stopping, or else you’ll fall down a gap.

Star Coin 2: Just after the first Star Coin, you’ll come across some sliding rocks that form stairsteps. Climb up them to jump over the ceiling and access a hidden pipe. Wall jump to collect the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: You’ll see the third Star Coin on your path, in the middle of a bunch of sliding rocks. Take your time and figure out the timing of the rocks so you can jump down and get it.

LAYER-CAKE DESERT — PIRANHA PLANTS ON ICE STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo

Star Coin 1: You’ll have to move fast to get the first Star Coin. You’ll see a pipe at the bottom of the screen with an ice block above it. Jump down and duck into the pipe before the block falls and crushes you. The Star Coin is right there.

Star Coin 2: The second Star Coin also requires timing. After the coin challenge are a few Piranha Plants on platforms. The last of these melts an ice block, sending it down onto a platform. Jump onto this block, then onto the block to the right (before the Piranha Plant melts it too), and you’ll see a pipe. It will take you to a room full of coins, Piranha Plants, and the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: Just after the second Star Coin is an area where you can jump onto ice blocks that Piranha Plants are melting. Stay fast and jump onto all of them before they melt, and eventually you’ll find the Star Coin.

https://www.polygon.com/new-super-mario-bros-u-deluxe-star-coins/2019/2/18/18217312/sparkling-waters

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe guide: Sparkling Waters Star Coins

Every Star Coin location in Sparkling Waters

By Kelly Hudson Sundberg  Feb 18, 2019, 8:58am EST

Share this story

Share this on Facebook (opens in new window)

Share this on Twitter (opens in new window)

SHARE

All sharing options

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe guide: Sparkling Waters Star Coins

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Part of New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe Star Coins guide

Sparkling Waters, as the name in New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe would imply, is a water level. You’ll encounter plenty of Cheep Cheeps, Urchins, Bloopers, and more. You’ll also find New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe’s first Ghost House level, the Haunted Shipwreck.

SPARKLING WATERS 1 — WATERSPOUT BEACH STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is easy to see — you’ll just need a little running start off the nearby water spout.

FEATURED VIDEOS FROM POLYGON

Building an F1 rig, Part 1 | GameStop sponsorship

Building an F1 rig, Part 1 | GameStop sponsorship

Star Coin 2: The second Star Coin is also easy to see, but it’s not so easy to reach. It’s under a pipe next to a water spout, and jumping to it is tricky. You could do that, but it’s much easier to use your Ice Flower powers to freeze the nearby Huckit Crab and toss the block of ice into the water below the Star Coin. It will float, giving you a platform to jump from.

Star Coin 3: Near the end of the level, you’ll see a series of water spouts and a POW Block. Throw the block, and the Star Coin (and many other coins) will rain from the sky. Make sure to time the throw so the coins drop when the spouts are up — once the water drops, the Star Coin sinks.

SPARKLING WATERS 2 — TROPICAL REFRESHER STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: Look for a collection of three pipes (red, then yellow, then red) pointing up and to the left. You’ll find the first Star Coin drifting along the side of these pipes.

Star Coin 2: Just after the checkpoint, you’ll see a platform with a giant Porcupuffer on either side. A trail of coins will appear and lead you below this platform, where a hidden P Switch will release coins and a Star Coin from above.

Star Coin 3: After you collect the second Star Coin, you’ll pass by a few giant Urchins before you see one guarding a pipe. Enter the pipe, and you’ll see that another giant Urchin is blocking your way. There are theoretically two ways to reach the Star Coin below the giant Urchin. The easier way requires that you have a power-up of some kind, so you can pass by the giant Urchin on the way down and back up, taking damage each time. The hard way requires a Starman, which you can get by entering the green pipe to the right of where you collected the second Star Coin. You’ll have to swim fast to make it before your Starman runs out.

SPARKLING WATERS TOWER — GIANT SKEWER TOWER STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is hidden just above and to the left of the first giant skewer you encounter. When the skewer resets, swim left into the gap the skewer slams into, then up to find the hidden Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: This Star Coin is easy to find — it’s at the top of the first set of skewers. A run and a jump will snag it for you.

Star Coin 3: You’ll see the final Star Coin easily, but collecting it is less obvious. You’ll see a place where two Dry Bones are patrolling an area with music note blocks on the floor and a skewer above you. Head to the far right side of the screen to uncover a hidden POW Block. Throwing it will dispatch the Dry Bones and send the Star Coin down onto some music note blocks. There’s no need to put yourself in the path of the skewer — just bump the music notes from below to collect the Star Coin.

SPARKLING WATERS GHOST HOUSE — HAUNTED SHIPWRECK STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: In the first area of the ship, you’ll see a Star Coin hanging just below the door. To reach it, you’ll need to ride the floating block to the left and jump off just below the platform to enter the hidden passage that leads to the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: In the second area of the ship, with the circling ghosts, the Star Coin is hanging in the air above the third circle.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin of the level is hanging in a chamber just above the entrance to the final area of the ship, with all the false walls. To reach it, go to the right of the door, then jump back to your left and wall jump to make it into the chamber. There are no Super Acorns in this level, but if you’re able to bring one in and keep the power-up for the entire level, a glide jump will make matters much easier here.

SPARKLING WATERS 3 — ABOVE THE CHEEP CHEEP SEAS STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is visible above a row of Donut Blocks. Let the scale platform to the left descend until you can jump out onto the Donut Blocks and collect the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: Just after the checkpoint, you’ll see three water spouts. Let the second spout drop you onto a row of Donut Blocks. Keep moving, and run to the right to collect the Star Coin, then let the spout lift you up.

Star Coin 3: You’ll need Yoshi for this one, or if you’re finding him hard to hang on to, use a Super Acorn before entering the level. You’ll pass by a series of scale platforms, the last of which is next to a pipe. Let this last platform descend, then use Yoshi’s flutter jump or a Super Acorn glide jump to reach the pipe. Inside the pipe, you’ll see several scale platforms and a POW Block. You might want to hop off Yoshi for greater maneuverability before you hit the POW Block, because it will send coins and the Star Coin down onto the scales, and you won’t have much time before the weight causes the scales to drop.

SPARKLING WATERS 4 — URCHIN SHOALS STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: Early in the level, you’ll see a giant Urchin below a row of Donut Blocks. Let the block to the right drop you, and then jump right as it falls to get to the space next to the giant Urchin. There is a brick here — hit it and jump up to get the Mini Mushroom. Head right and jump in the water — as Mini Mario, you can run on the water and collect the first Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: As soon as you collect the first Star Coin, a trail of coins will appear leading up the side of a water spout. Use Mini Mario to run up the spout and into the pipe — the Star Coin is easy to collect here.

Star Coin 3: After the checkpoint, you’ll pass through an area with lots of giant Urchins. After this, you’ll come to an area with smaller Urchins riding on water spouts, with a red Koopa Troopa patrolling above them. Knock out the Koopa Troopa and carry its shell below the platform, where the Urchins are. When the Urchins’ spouts are all up, throw the shell to take out the Urchins, then walk on the cleared spouts to reach a pipe. Inside the pipe will be two giant Urchins, coins, and your final Star Coin.

SPARKLING WATERS 5 — DRAGONEEL’S UNDERSEA GROTTO STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is easy to spot near the top of the screen. Take it slow so you can avoid Dragoneel.

Star Coin 2: The second Star Coin is similarly easy to spot, and it’s encased in bricks to slow you down. Dragoneel is your big challenge in this area as well.

Star Coin 3: After the checkpoint, look for a red pipe in the ground. It will take you to a room with the final Star Coin and two smaller Dragoneel.

SPARKLING WATERS CASTLE — LARRY’S TORPEDO CASTLE STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: After the grates in the first area, you’ll see two question blocks. Climb on them and wall jump up and to the left to collect your first Star Coin (or, to make it easier, bring a Super Acorn into the level and use your glide jump). Make sure to time your jump around the fire that’s spouting right next to the Star Coin!

Star Coin 2: Just after the checkpoint is the next Star Coin. You’ll have to time the torpedoes flying through this area, and swim fast!

Star Coin 3: In the third area of the castle is another set of moving grates and fire spouts. Ride the center grate up to access the Star Coin hidden behind the wall.

SPARKLING WATERS — SKYWARD STALK STAR COINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin will be to the left of the stalk, above a platform on the left wall. Start looking for it after the red coin challenge.

Star Coin 2: Just above where you found the first Star Coin is a wall with a pipe sticking out of it at an angle. Get on top of this wall, and from there you can jump up to a platform where the second Star Coin is hidden in some clouds.

Star Coin 3: After you get the second Star Coin, work your way right — the final Star Coin is above another wall with a slanted pipe. Your Super Acorn glide jump will make collecting this Star Coin a snap.

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe guide: Frosted Glacier Star Coins

Every Star Coin location in Frosted Glacier

By Kelly Hudson Sundberg  Feb 18, 2019, 8:58am EST

Share this story

Share this on Facebook (opens in new window)

Share this on Twitter (opens in new window)

SHARE

All sharing options

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe Frosted Glacier Star Coins guide

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Part of New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe Star Coins guide

In this guide, we’ll show you the locations of every Star Coin in New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe’s Frosted Glacier world.

FROSTED GLACIER 1 — SPINNING-STAR SKY

 GRID VIEW

1 of 7

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first coin is visible above a large spinning star. Use your Super Acorn jump to glide to it.

FEATURED VIDEOS FROM POLYGON

Awful Squad Reunion

00:00

140:35

Awful Squad Reunion

Star Coin 2: The second Star Coin is above an area with a Piranha Plant. Jump on the large spinning star to the left of the plant, and use your Super Acorn glide jump to reach a hidden tunnel in the rock above. When you come out the other side, use another glide jump to get on top of the rock, where you’ll find the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: After the green coin challenge, there’s an area filled with Munchers and spinning stars above them. Use your Super Acorn glide jump to get to a hidden room next to the Munchers, where you can hit a brick to get a Starman. While you’re invincible, run across the next section of Munchers to the right and into a hidden room with the final Star Coin.

FROSTED GLACIER 2 — COOLIGAN FIELDS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is easy to find, but the Cooligans sliding around under it make it a little tougher to collect. Stand on the bricks above them and take them out with Ice Flower blasts before heading down to collect the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: After the red coin challenge, you’ll see two pipes with bricks and question mark blocks between them. Break a few bricks to get into this area, jump on the question block above, and use it to wall jump between the pipes to reach a Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin is visible high above the level. There are a few platforms and bricks you can jump on — it’s tricky, especially with the Cooligans sliding beneath you, but you can do it. Hold down A as you jump on a Cooligan to give yourself a boost up to the lowest platform.

FROSTED GLACIER TOWER — FREEZING-RAIN TOWER

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first coin is easy to see — it’s on the left side of the second diagonal platform. Watch out for the icicles!

Star Coin 2: The second Star Coin will appear during the rising platform sequence. You’ll have to be slightly ahead of the platform, or it will crush you as it rises. To get some distance, you can wall jump up the sides of the chamber after it narrows, or just make a run for it and take damage from the Dry Bones in the area.

Star Coin 3: After collecting the second Star Coin, you’ll see a trail of coins leading to the right. Instead of following it, head to the far left, where you can enter a hidden room and collect the final Star Coin.

FROSTED GLACIER 3 — PRICKLY GOOMBAS!

 GRID VIEW

1 of 6

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see a pipe leading up, with a Prickly Goomba and three regular Goombas guarding it. Jump up near the Prickly Goomba to direct the fire of the nearby Piranha Plant so the Goomba will pop out. Once the pipe entrance is clear, you can head in. This room has three hidden coin blocks on each side. Jump off of them to get the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: You’ll need Yoshi for this Star Coin. Luckily, you can find him right after the checkpoint. Keep moving on the rolling hill to find a hidden room with Yoshi’s egg inside. (Before you grab Yoshi, you may want to get rid of the Fire Bro on the right — it makes things easier.) Jump onto the Fire Bro’s brick platform, and from here, flutter jump with Yoshi to a platform where you can take a pipe. The next room is filled with Prickly Goombas, but Yoshi can walk on top of them. A simple jump, and the Star Coin is yours.

Star Coin 3: The third Star Coin is easy to see, above a Fire Bro and some Prickly Goombas. If you still have Yoshi, you can flutter jump to the Star Coin; if you’ve lost him, you can run and jump off the ice to the right.

FROSTED GLACIER 4 — SCALING THE MOUNTAINSIDE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The easiest way to reach all the Star Coins in this level is to bring a Super Acorn with you. The first one is visible above a series of scale platforms. Stand on one side of the scale until the other side is at the top, then jump up quickly to collect the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: The second Star Coin is above an area with many floating coins and three Bullet Bill cannons. If you don’t have the Super Acorn, you’ll need to jump off the flying Bullet Bills to get high enough to reach it.

Star Coin 3: Look for a set of scale platforms, followed by a rock with an upside-down Bullet Bill cannon embedded in it. There’s a small ledge just above the cannon. Your mission is to get on it. Once you do, you’ll discover a hidden room with the final Star Coin.

FROSTED GLACIER 5 — ICICLE CAVERNS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: Just past two bricks and a question block, you’ll see a large icicle hanging ominously. Run past it before it drops, and you’ll find yourself in a hidden room with the first Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: A little past the checkpoint, you’ll encounter an Ice Bro below a pipe. The only way to enter the pipe is to boost yourself off the Ice Bro as you jump on him (hold A as you jump). The pipe will take you to a secret room with the Star Coin. Watch out for the giant icicles!

Star Coin 3: Toward the end of the level, you’ll reach a point where you can simply run through, avoiding icicles. Build up your speed and beat the final icicle from blocking the last pipe in the level. Take this pipe to come out by the final Star Coin.

FROSTED GLACIER GHOST HOUSE — SWAYING GHOST HOUSE

 GRID VIEW

Star Coin 1: You’ll see the Star Coin in the first room. Use the P Switch to walk through the wall above the Star Coin on the left side. The P Switch resets after completing its sequence, so you can take your time here.

Star Coin 2: You’ll eventually enter a room with a door underneath you — bricks block it, so hit the P Switch on the other side of the room, then come back to access the door. Inside, you’ll find two sets of coins that move when you try to collect them. Hit the nearby P Switch so they turn into blocks, then dash through the room, avoiding the Boos. At the end of the room, the blocks will stop. Use them to collect the Star Coin hanging above. You’ll have to be quick, so instead of jumping on the blocks, try wall-jumping off the top one — it’s a faster way up.

Star Coin 3: This one is trickiest of all, not least because you have to access the secret exit door to get it. To do that, in the room where the row of coins/bricks rise as you jump, keep jumping past the exit door and go right into the ceiling, where there’s a hidden door. Go through it, and you’ll see a room filled with bricks, coins, and a P Switch — and the room is moving! Hit the P Switch and dash through the room — at the end, if the P Switch is still active, you’ll see two lines of bricks you can wall jump up to grab your final Star Coin.

FROSTED GLACIER CASTLE — WENDY’S SHIFTING CASTLE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 7

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is hiding under a Thwomp right before the checkpoint. Wait for it to fall, then wall jump up to get it. (To make this easier, you could bring a Super Acorn into the level and glide jump to it.)

Star Coin 2: You’ll see two Thwimps circling bricks. (If you fall through the floor into a hidden room full of coins, you’ll know you’re in the right place.) Get on the second brick (duck when Thwimp is flying overhead) and jump straight up to reveal a hidden coin block. Get on top of this block to access a pipe in the ceiling. The room it takes you to is full of Thwimps — avoid them, take your time, and claim the Star Coin at the end of the room.

Star Coin 3: Near the end of the level (just before the series of giant Thwomps), you’ll see a shifting platform with a pipe at the other side. That’s your cue — let the platform drop you as it shifts down into its base. Stay right next to the pillar, and you’ll drop onto a pipe. It takes you to a room with raising and lowering platforms and a giant Thwomp — when he comes down, you’ll see the final Star Coin.

FROSTED GLACIER — FLIPRUS LAKE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see a red Koopa Troopa on a set of three bricks that are moving up and down. Take out the Koopa Troopa from below, then get on the blocks and ride them to their highest point. One jump and the Star Coin is yours.

Star Coin 2: You’ll come to a point where Fliprus are riding up and down on moving platforms. You’ll see a platform above one of the Fliprus — take it out, then jump to the platform (this is easier if you freeze the Fliprus with your Ice Flower power). From this platform, you can access a pipe — take it to a room with two Fliprus, coins, and the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: You’ll see a wooden platform floating in the water next to a rock wall. Take out the Fliprus next to the platform so you won’t have to worry about its snowballs. Let the platform sink and then pop back up — at its highest point, jump toward the rock wall, and you will be able to enter a hidden tunnel containing the final Star Coin.

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe guide: Soda Jungle Star Coins

How to find every Star Coin in Soda Jungle

By Kelly Hudson Sundberg  Feb 18, 2019, 8:58am EST

Share this story

Share this on Facebook (opens in new window)

Share this on Twitter (opens in new window)

SHARE

All sharing options

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe guide: Soda Jungle Star Coins

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Part of New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe Star Coins guide

In this guide, we’ll show you the locations of every Star Coin in New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe’s Soda Jungle world.

SODA JUNGLE AIRSHIP — THE MIGHTY CANNONSHIP

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is easy to see, down a rope ladder near the beginning of the level. Move quickly so you don’t get crushed by the moving ship.

FEATURED VIDEOS FROM POLYGON

The Great Game: The Making of Spycraft Teaser Trailer

The never-before-told story of how a former KGB Major General and an ex-CIA director worked together… on a video game.

Star Coin 2: The second Star Coin is just before the checkpoint and requires a bit of timing — repeated jumps on the screw platform below it, plus a jump on a flying cannonball to boost you up to the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: In the second area of the ship (the one filled with water), you’ll see the final Star Coin surrounded by bricks. Avoid the Bull’s-Eye Bills, break the bricks, and collect your prize.

SODA JUNGLE 1 — JUNGLE OF THE GIANTS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see the first Star Coin at the bottom of a series of three platforms, seemingly blocked off by bricks you can’t break. At the top of the platforms is a giant Koopa Troopa. Knock it out and send its shell flying — it will break the bricks, giving you access to the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: Past the checkpoint, you’ll come to a small platform with an arrow signpost on it. Next to this is a giant Koopa Troopa on a regular-sized pipe. Take out the Koopa Troopa, then take the pipe to a room where you’ll see bricks, giant Goombas, a P Switch, and the Star Coin. Hit the switch so the Goombas fall to the ground, then jump on them to boost yourself up to the Star Coin. If you’re having trouble reaching it, leave a few coins in place to turn back into bricks once the P Switch wears off, then use the bricks as a platform to reach the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin requires speed and timing. Near the end of the level, you’ll see a Koopa Troopa walking on top of lots of bricks. Take it out and knock the shell into the bricks. This will open up several question blocks, one of which contains a Starman. As soon as you get the Starman, start running as fast as you can to the end of the level. You’ll come to Koopa Troopas on raised bricks — it’ll require some big jumps, but if you reach them all and take them all out with your Starman, you can jump to the final Star Coin and the end of the level.

SODA JUNGLE 2 — BRIDGE OVER POISONED WATER

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see the first Star Coin above a rolling log near the water. Keep jumping as you approach it so you don’t fall in.

Star Coin 2: Very soon after collecting the first Star Coin, you’ll see a pipe high above a large log. You’ll find some hidden coin blocks if you jump up from the very center of the log, then more coin blocks jumping up from those, and a third lone coin block above those. This will get you into the pipe, which will take you to an area with coins and the Star Coin. Hit the nearby bricks to find a P Switch that you can use to turn the coins to bricks and reach the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: You’ll come to an area where bricks pop up out of the water to make a platform for you. Walk along this platform until you see the Star Coin emerge from the water. It won’t stay long, so snag it while you can … but if you do miss it, it comes back when the bricks do.

SODA JUNGLE 3 — BRAMBALL WOODS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe

Star Coin 1: You’ll see the first Star Coin early in the level, among some brick columns that pop up. Take out the Bramball guarding it, then slide down to snag it and wall jump back up.

Star Coin 2: Almost immediately after you get the first Star Coin, you’ll come to two platforms next to a wall with that telltale “there’s something hidden here” indentation. Get onto the lower platform to reach the hidden room, which contains your second Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin is visible beneath two rows of Donut Blocks. Drop down and work your way over to claim it.

SODA JUNGLE TOWER — SNAKE-BLOCK TOWER

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is right in the path of the large snake block. A quick jump, and it’s yours.

Star Coin 2: After the red coin challenge, your large snake block will be joined with a smaller version. Stay with the small snake block, and it will take you to the second Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: In the second room, your large snake block will again be joined with a small snake block. When the two separate, take the smaller snake block to the third Star Coin, then quickly jump back to the large snake block. (A Super Acorn glide jump is helpful here.)

SODA JUNGLE GHOST HOUSE — WHICH-WAY LABYRINTH

 GRID VIEW

1 of 15

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: To get the first Star Coin, in the main room with five doors, take the top right door. You’ll find yourself in a room with a platform that slowly sinks and three Broozers trying to attack you. (Unlike normal Boos, these you can stun and even kill them. Jumping on them three times takes them out.) Let the platform sink a bit, and you’ll see the Star Coin on the left. Let the Broozer in the room with the Star Coin break out so you can access the area, then head in and grab it. The sinking platform will rise up when you’re not standing on it, blocking off the way you came in, but you can leave the Star Coin chamber by jumping straight up to a hidden platform.

Star Coin 2: In the main room with five doors, take the lower right door. You’ll be in dark space full of Scaredy Rats. Grab Glowing Baby Yoshi from the nearby question block — he’s not essential, but he’ll make dealing with the Scaredy Rats much easier. Work your way to the end of the room, and you’ll see a door. Instead of taking this door, head past it and jump against the wall to access a hidden area. It will lead you to a different door, which takes you to a room filled with staircases. Work your way to the very top of the room, where you’ll see a door — don’t take that one, either. Instead, staind against the wall to the left of the door and jump straight up — you will hit a hidden coin block that you can then stand on to get over the wall and access yet another hidden door. In this room, you will see coins, blocks, and a P Switch — hit that switch, clean up all the coins, and collect your Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: The path to the third Star Coin is also the way to this level’s secret exit. In the main room with five doors, take the lower left door. You’ll find yourself in a room with a floor that rises as long as you’re standing on it. Grab Glowing Baby Yoshi from the first question block to make avoiding the swarming Boos easier. When the floor reaches the door at the top of the room, keep going and move to the left — you’ll go up through a hidden area in the ceiling and reach a platform. (Make sure you jump to the platform instead of letting the floor rise to it — you want to get off the floor so it sinks and doesn’t crush you.) There’s a door on this platform — be ready, because when you come out, you’ll only have a second or two before the floor collapses under you. Move to the right side of the room, and when you reach the third collapsing floor, walk into the wall to reach a hidden area with the final Star Coin.

SODA JUNGLE 4 — PAINTED SWAMPLAND

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: Look for a yellow pipe among all the green pipes. Wait out the Piranha Plant, and take the yellow pipe to a room filled with circling Boos and the Star Coin. Time your jump and grab it.

Star Coin 2: The second Star Coin is just before the checkpoint. It’s easy to see above three green pipes, but the pipes all sink, so move fast to jump up to it.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin is also easy to see, above some blue pipes with Boos circling. Take your time and wait for the gap in the Boos’ circle to line up with the movement of the pipe.

SODA JUNGLE 5 — DEEPSEA RUINS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: In the first area, while you are still above water, you will see two Stone-Eyes facing each other. The Star Coin is in the water beneath them.

Star Coin 2: After the checkpoint, you’ll be in an underwater area where you need to move up. The Star Coin is on the right side of the room, guarded by circling Boos.

Star Coin 3: At the top of the room, a Stone-Eye will fall over, giving you a platform to jump to a pipe. Instead of taking it, jump to the other side of the Stone-Eye, where you’ll find both the final Star Coin and a pipe taking you to the level’s secret exit.

SODA JUNGLE 6 — SEESAW BRIDGE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 8

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: After the random power-up block, look for a pipe sticking up out of the water, seemingly into the ground. It turns out there’s a hidden area you can access to take the pipe. The area with the star Coin is tricky, but you can do it. Jump onto the edge of the rotating seesaw platform to reach it.

Star Coin 2: You’ll see this Star Coin halfway submerged in the poisoned waters. To collect it, knock out the nearby red Koopa Troopa and launch its shell off the seesaw platform.

Star Coin 3: You’ll see a series of three pipes, the last of which has a question block over it. Take this last pipe to a room where you’ll immediately hit a P Switch. Jump to hit many hidden coin blocks below the Star Coin, then head all the way to the left and jump on top of the blocks before the P Switch runs out. Use the blocks you’ve hit to reach the Star Coin.

SODA JUNGLE 7 — WIGGLER STAMPEDE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see the first Star Coin next to a giant Wiggler stomping through the poisoned water. Jump on its back and ride it to the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: Just after the first Star Coin, you’ll see another giant Wiggler stomping around in the muck. Above it is a platform and a series of three bricks. Jump on the Wiggler and use it to boost you up — first to hit the center brick, witch will turn into a vine; then to jump up to the platform. Climb the vine, and you will be taken to an area with many coins and question blocks, some of which contain other vines. Climb these to reach the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: After the checkpoint, you will have to ride a giant Wiggler for quite some time. The final Star Coin is part of this sequence — you can find it between two platforms, and you can jump down to it as the Wiggler emerges from the water.

SODA JUNGLE CASTLE — IGGY’S VOLCANIC CASTLE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 6

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see the first Star Coin under a platform between waves of lava. The easiest way to get it is to freeze a Dry Bones with your Ice Flower power and send the block of ice sliding into the Star Coin. If you don’t have the Ice Flower power-up, you can reach it yourself with carefully timed jumps.

Star Coin 2: You’ll see two green pipes and a yellow pipe in the ceiling. Jump on a Dry Bones to boost you into the second green pipe. You’ll find yourself in a room with a couple of Dry Bones, some coins, and a POW Block. Use the POW Block to bring down the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: Just before the doors at the end of the level, look for a green pipe that’s almost entirely submerged by lava. When the lava is at its lowest point, jump down and take it to a room where several Super Dry Bones are parading around in the lava. Bounce off their heads to collect the Star Coin.

SODA JUNGLE — FLIGHT OF THE PARA-BEETLES

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: This first Star Coin will appear soon after you leave the ground behind. To get to it, jump onto one of the Para-Beetles flying below it and let the Para-Beetle fly up into it.

Star Coin 2: The second Star Coin will show up soon after the first platform mid-level. The best strategy here may be to fly a Para-Beetle above the incoming King Bill, then jump down to a Heavy Para-Beetle, which will sink to the coin.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin will require some patience and timing. Toward the end of the level, you must use four Para-Beetles to avoid a King Bill and reach a pipe. Instead of landing on top of the red pipe and taking the green pipe, you must fly a Para-Beetle next to the red pipe so you can enter it. To control the Para-Beetle’s height, keep jumping when it is at the height you want, so it will lower a bit. Enter the red pipe, and the Star Coin and the end of the level await

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe guide: Rock-Candy Mines Star Coins

How to find every Star Coin in Rock-Candy Mines

By Kelly Hudson Sundberg  Feb 18, 2019, 8:58am EST

Share this story

Share this on Facebook (opens in new window)

Share this on Twitter (opens in new window)

SHARE

All sharing options

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe Rock-Candy Mines Star Coins

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Part of New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe Star Coins guide

In this guide, we’ll show you the locations of every Star Coin in New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe’s Rock-Candy Mines world.

ROCK-CANDY MINES 1 — FUZZY CLIFFTOP

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll need Yoshi for all the Star Coins in this level. The first Star Coin is easily visible above a Fuzzy. Use Yoshi to jump on the Fuzzy and collect the Star Coin.

FEATURED VIDEOS FROM POLYGON

Awful Squad Reunion

Awful Squad Reunion

Star Coin 2: Just after the checkpoint, you’ll see a pipe near a windmill-like platform. Use Yoshi’s flutter jump to make it to the pipe, then take it to a room filled with Fuzzies and the Star Coin. Use Yoshi to jump on the Fuzzies so you can reach the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: You’ll see the final Star Coin at the end of an area filled with Piranha Plants. Use Yoshi to jump on the plants below and eat the plants above, then snag your Star Coin.

ROCK-CANDY MINES 2 — PORCUPUFFER FALLS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 8

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: Early in the level, you’ll come across a signpost with a down arrow. Instead of jumping into the water, jump across to the stone ledge. Jump up to reveal a hidden coin block and use that to get over the wall. Keep heading right, and you’ll drop to the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: After the checkpoint, you’ll be directed to enter the water between two red pipes. Before you do so, grab a Koopa Troopa shell and bring it with you. Use the shell to clear away the Urchins blocking the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: Near the end of the level, you’ll come across a Roulette Block. Jump on the block, and you’ll be able to jump up to reveal a hidden vine. Climb the vine to a room filled with coins, then drop back down to reach the final Star Coin.

ROCK-CANDY MINES TOWER — GRINDING-STONE TOWER

 GRID VIEW

1 of 6

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see a green horizontal pipe moving in and out — get on top of the pipe and move left to enter a hidden area with a Mini Mushroom. Keep working your way up as Mini Mario, and you’ll see a tiny pipe on the left. Take it, and avoid the Grrroll to get your first Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: After you exit the room with the first Star Coin, you’ll see a Grrroll rolling along a platform with a safe spot on the right. Head there and keep moving right to reveal a hidden area where you can jump up to a Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: You’ll need to stay Mini Mario for this, so avoid any Super Mushroom power-ups that would make you grow back to normal size. Work your way up, and eventually you will see a mini pipe on the right. You’ll enter a room with Grrrolls, but they’re easy to avoid — work your way to the top and claim your final Star Coin.

ROCK-CANDY MINES 3 — WADDLEWING’S NEST

 GRID VIEW

1 of 6

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: To the right of one of the seesaw platforms, you will see a group of green pipes with a red pipe at the end. Take the red pipe to a room with four columns of bricks in the ground. Ground pound the second column from the left, and you’ll have your Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: To the left of the last seesaw platform in the first area, you’ll find a hidden P Switch. Hit it and follow the trail of blue coins to a hidden area on the right side of the screen. Jump on the music note blocks and get up to the spot where the Star Coin is — it’s blocked by bricks, so you’ve got to get there before the P Switch runs out.

Star Coin 3: In the area with the descending platform, you’ll see the Star Coin on the right. Wall jump to reach it.

ROCK-CANDY MINES 4 — LIGHT BLOCKS, DARK TOWER

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is on the right side of the tower almost as soon as you begin to climb it.

Star Coin 2: As you climb the tower, you’ll see a yellow pipe on the right side. Go through the pipe to enter an area with several Koopa Paratroopas and the Star Coin. You’ll need to jump on the Koopa Paratroopas to reach it.

Star Coin 3: Just before the pipe leading to the end of the level, you’ll see a Fire Bro. The Star Coin is right behind him. Take him out and claim your prize.

ROCK-CANDY MINES 5 — WALKING PIRANHA PLANTS!

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: Early in the level, you’ll come to an area with several walking Piranha Plants and one red Koopa Troopa. Launch the shell at the Piranhas — it will take them out and hit a hidden brick below. Slide under the barrier and collect the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: You’ll need a Super Acorn to reach the second Star Coin. You’ll get one in the cave where a walking Piranha Plant is on a moving platform below a question block. Once you have the Super Acorn, backtrack to the entrance to that cave and do a glide jump up through the gap in the ceiling. The Star Coin is far overhead to your left, and you’ll need to do another glide jump to reach it. To make it easier, hit the P Switch and jump off the nearby blocks.

Star Coin 3: This Star Coin also requires a Super Acorn, so if you don’t have one, hope that the Roulette Block nearby will give you one. Just past the checkpoint, you’ll see the final Star Coin under a low rocky outcropping. To get it, you’ll have to head down to the low platform to the right, jump out into the gap, and glide as far as you can — then, use your glide jump to boost yourself through the Star Coin and up to the platform.

ROCK-CANDY MINES TOWER — SCREWTOP TOWER

 GRID VIEW

1 of 6

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see a yellow pipe with a signpost on it. Take the screw platform here all the way to its end, then jump up to reveal two hidden coin blocks connecting the pipe to the nearby platform. This will allow you to enter the pipe. When you do, get up to the platform and use the POW Block to eliminate the Bony Beetles and bring down the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: You’ll find this one at the end of a screw platform — pretty straightforward. No tricks except avoiding the Fire Bar here.

Star Coin 3: When you get to the top of the tower, there will be a Sumo Bro on a platform. Take him out, then climb on the platform and jump up. You will find a series of hidden coin blocks. Use them to reach the final Star Coin.

ROCK-CANDY MINES 6 — THRILLING SPINE COASTER

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: Early in the level, the coaster will screech for the first time, and its head will pop up, illuminating the first Star Coin. Jump up and grab it.

Star Coin 2: The coaster will pass by several Spike Tops, then two sections of coins you can jump to, then a few coins right in the coaster’s path. After these coins, look for the Star Coin overhead.

Star Coin 3: After the second Star Coin, you’ll come to a question block at the top of a hill. It contains a Starman. Collect it and then focus on not getting dunked into the poison waters. At the end of the line is a question block. As long as you are still invincible from the last Starman when you hit it, it will contain another Starman. Collect it and then go through the pipe. At the other end you’ll find yet another question block — and, if you’re still invincible, another Starman. Collect this and use your invincibility to run across the nearby Munchers to the Star Coin.

ROCK-CANDY MINES 7 — SHIFTING-FLOOR CAVE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is right along your path — just time the shifting floors so you can collect it.

Star Coin 2: After the checkpoint, you’ll come to a question block with a green Koopa Troopa under it, followed by a rising and falling column and a Piranha Plant above the ceiling. You’ll notice some coins above the Piranha Plant. Ride the column up to the ceiling and jump to break some blocks and get above the ceiling. You’ll find plenty of coins and a Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: The final star Coin is in a seemingly inaccessible place, just below the pipe to exit the level. To collect it, backtrack until you find a Koopa Troopa, knock it out, and bring its shell with you to toss through the Star Coin.

ROCK-CANDY MINES CASTLE — ROY’S CONVEYOR CASTLE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see the first Star Coin near the beginning of the level, but you’ll need to move fast to claim it. Wait until the first block drops from the conveyor above, blocking the gap to the lava, then rush through before the second block drops and blocks the opening.

Star Coin 2: Just before the checkpoint, you’ll ride blocks through the lava and pass through three spaces where you can jump up. The first two spaces have coins. The third has nothing — or does it? Wall jump up to find a hidden pipe, which takes you to a room with the Star Coin. Dash across the room, jumping to keep the conveyor from slowing your momentum, and collect the Star Coin before the block above can fall on it.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin requires a second pass at the castle. Completing level 6, Thrilling Spine Coaster, will open an alternate route to the castle, which you can access while the red snake blocks are extended. Taking the red blocks will lead you to the back of the castle, where you will spawn to the right of the door to the boss. Head left and hit the question switch to drop blocks into the lava, then run and grab the Star Coin before the final block drops and closes your path.

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe guide: Meringue Clouds Star Coins

How to find every Star Coin in Meringue Clouds

By Kelly Hudson Sundberg  Feb 18, 2019, 8:58am EST

Share this story

Share this on Facebook (opens in new window)

Share this on Twitter (opens in new window)

SHARE

All sharing options

Meringue Clouds Star Coins

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Part of New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe Star Coins guide

In this guide, we’ll show you the locations of every Star Coin in New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe’s Meringue Clouds world.

MERINGUE CLOUDS 1 — LAND OF FLYING BLOCKS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: Stay toward the front of the cloud platform — this will give you the most time to execute the moves you’ll need to get the Star Coins in this level. Just before you pass under an area filled with pipes, you’ll see a question block fly up with the other bricks. Hit it and collect the springboard that appears. When you fly under the pipes, use the springboard to bounce up and take the red pipe in the corner. The pipe will take you to an area that looks deserted, but when you start to move, blocks and coins will fly into place, giving you a path to reach the first Star Coin.

FEATURED VIDEOS FROM POLYGON

I love notetaking games and I don't care if that sounds boring

I love notetaking games and I don't care if that sounds boring

Star Coin 2: As soon as you emerge from the pipe after collecting the first Star Coin, you’ll see the second one floating overhead. Get onto the row of bricks nearby — it’s not quite close enough to reach, but more blocks will fly into place, allowing you to reach the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin appears while you are riding the third cloud platform. Blocks fly up to block it from you, so get on top of the blocks and ground pound to access the Star Coin, then jump to break out before the cloud platform leaves you behind.

MERINGUE CLOUDS 2 — SEESAW SHROOMS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is easiest if you have Yoshi, but he’s not essential. You’ll see the Star Coin on a seesaw platform early in the level — it’s possible to reach it on your own if you get enough of a running start from the seesaw to the left, or you can use Yoshi’s flutter jump.

Star Coin 2: Just after the checkpoint, a Lakitu will appear. Work your way a few platforms over until you find one high enough so you can jump on its head. Around this point, you’ll notice a circle of coins in the sky. Defeat the Lakitu and use its cloud to rise up to the coins — you’ll also find the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: You’ll need Yoshi for the final Star Coin. After the spot with the second Star Coin, you’ll reach an area with expanding and retracting platforms and several Fuzzies. Eventually, you’ll come to a platform ringed by two Fuzzies and a pipe overhead. Use Yoshi to bounce off a Fuzzy and into the pipe, where you’ll see the Star Coin high overhead. Use Yoshi’s flutter jump. Then, when he’s at his highest point, leap off his back to reach the Star Coin.

MERINGUE CLOUDS 3 — SWITCHBACK HILL

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see this Star Coin under a platform. Use the platform headed left to collect it, ducking to avoid the Bullet Bills.

Star Coin 2: You’ll see a long platform with a down arrow next to a rocky outcropping. There’s a telltale indentation in the rock that says something is hidden there. Jump on the platform and wall jump up to find a hidden pipe. Take it to a room with a Star Coin, plus many platforms and Bullet Bills.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin requires timing and speed. You’ll see a POW Block on a platform. Take it down and throw it underneath the right-arrow platform. You’ll bring the Star Coin down onto the platform, which will start moving. The next part is tricky, because you have to keep up with that platform. Take the platforms as quickly as possible and dodge the Bullet Bills — eventually, the platform will slide under a wall, and push the Star Coin off. Catch it as it falls.

MERINGUE CLOUDS TOWER — SLIDE LIFT TOWER

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll need a Super Acorn for this Star Coin. At the top of the first room in the tower, you will see the Star Coin above a Fire Bro. Take him out, then glide jump to break the bricks and grab the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: You’ll also need a Super Acorn here. In the middle of the second room, you’ll see a signpost pointing right. On the opposite wall is a hidden area with coins and another hidden area with a pipe. Use your glide jump to reach the pipe. The next area will require you to jump on more sliding blocks — time your moves and use the Super Acorn’s glide to help you reach the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: Just above where you got the second Star Coin is an area with a huge Fire Bar. You’ll enter on the right side — head to the wall and jump up to reveal a hidden Fire Flower. Climb on the block and jump again to reveal a hidden coin block. From here, you can access a hidden pipe. The next room is filled with sliding blocks and looks overwhelming. Note the nooks in the walls where you can hide if you need a break. Just keep working your way up and left, and the Star Coin will be yours.

MERINGUE CLOUDS GHOST HOUSE — SPINNING SPIRIT HOUSE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 8

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: In the main grid room, when you make it to the second floor, take the door on the left. You’ll find yourself in a room with a Broozer — don’t hurt him! Just avoid him and lure him to the sides, where he’ll punch out boxes filled with coins. Eventually, you can lead him to break some blocks and clear the way to the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: After you exit the room with the first Star Coin, you can take a springboard to the third floor of the main grid room. The Star Coin is in the room to the right (you’ll have to go through the room to the left to gain access to it, though). You’ll enter the room on a rising platform with Boos spinning around you. Eventually, you’ll see the Star Coin. As you move toward it, it will jump to the other side of the room. Collect it before the platform rises up and hides it, but if you can’t make it, you can return to this room and try again.

Star Coin 3: After you exit the room with the second Star Coin, you’ll be back in the main grid room. At the right end of this floor, you can drop back down to the second floor. Grab the springboard and bring it one square to the left — you can use it to jump to the third floor, then drop to a hidden room on the left side of the second floor. Once there, you’ll find a P Switch. Follow the trail of blue coins to a hidden area (it’ll be blocked off by bricks if you don’t get there before the P Switch runs out). Use the springboard to collect your final Star Coin.

MERINGUE CLOUDS 4 — BOUNCY CLOUD BOOMERANGS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 7

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see a group of four green pipes with a Boomerang Bro nearby. In the gap in the middle of the four pipes, you will just be able to make out a Star Coin in the clouds. Slide down and snag it, then wall jump back up.

Star Coin 2: Just after the spot where you got the first Star Coin is a long row of bricks and question blocks. Ground pound a brick to land on one of the bouncy clouds below, then hit the question blocks. One of them will spawn a vine. Climb up the vine and make your way through this new area until you spot a POW Block. Throw it to bring the Star Coin down, but grab it before it rolls off the bouncy clouds.

Star Coin 3: Just after the checkpoint, you will see a stone cliff with a green pipe below. Get down onto the pipe and head left to find a second, hidden pipe. Take it, and you will be shot through a line of coins and see the Star Coin above. Jump on the bouncy cloud and avoid the Boomerang Bro to get it.

MERINGUE CLOUDS 5 — A QUICK DIP IN THE SKY

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is easy to see, circling two giant Piranha Plants.

Star Coin 2: The second Star Coin is also easy to see, but getting it is less obvious. It appears to be buried in a cliff under a platform — but on the side of that cliff is the telltale indentation of a hidden spot. Jump at the cliff wall to discover a path to the Star Coin. For a bonus, enter the pipe below — it will shoot you through the Star Coin and take you further up the level.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin is near the end of the level, surrounded by two swarming Fuzzies. You’ll have to shoot them or take damage to claim your prize.

MERINGUE CLOUDS 6 — SNAKING ABOVE MIST VALLEY

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: Early in the level, you will see a P Switch in the sky (a Foo will try to blow mist to cover it). Hit it and follow the blue coins to the first Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: Soon after the first Star Coin, you will jump off the snake block and onto a set of two grassy platforms with bricks between them. To the right of these platforms you will see the second Star Coin. When the snake block rises up to it, jump up to get it.

Star Coin 3: After the checkpoint, the snake block will head straight down a line of coins. That’s your cue to get ready for the final Star Coin by knocking out one of the Koopa Paratroopas and grabbing its shell. You’ll see the Star Coin, but the snake block is in your way. After you pass it, throw the Koopa shell at it to collect it.

MERINGUE CLOUDS CASTLE — LUDWIG’S CLOCKWORK CASTLE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: After passing a Big Dry Bones and a Fire Bro, you’ll come to an area with two clockwork platforms smashing together. Above these platforms, you will see a nook with some coins. Wall jump up that nook to find a hidden Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: You will see a POW Block above a Sumo Bro. Jump on his head and hit the POW Block, which will bring down the second Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: Just before the end of the level, you will see the Star Coin underneath a long platform that a clockwork block is running back and forth on. Move fast and get the Star Coin before the block gets you.

MERINGUE CLOUDS AIRSHIP — BOARDING THE AIRSHIP

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll spend a couple of minutes dodging the airship’s mechanical hand as it slaps onto the ground. After a few slaps, it’ll take a short break. When this happens, get as far forward as you can. The robot hand will come back as a fist and punch down, destroying the landscape. Keep ahead of it, and after a few punches you’ll see the Star Coin in a nook underneath a hill. Get in, get it, and as soon as more land appears for you to jump to, get out.

Star Coin 2: After the first checkpoint, you’ll jump onto a platform you can make move by tilting your controller. The second Star Coin is close — you’ll see it above three fire spouts. Time your movements and claim your prize.

Star Coin 3: You’ll need a Super Acorn to reach the final Star Coin. After the coin challenge, the camera will pan up and you’ll be able to see it on a platform above you. Use a glide jump to jump up and collect it.

New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe guide: Peach’s Castle Star Coins

How to find every Star Coin in Peach’s Castle

By Kelly Hudson Sundberg  Feb 18, 2019, 8:58am EST

Share this story

Share this on Facebook (opens in new window)

Share this on Twitter (opens in new window)

SHARE

All sharing options

Peach’s Castle Star Coins guide

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Part of New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe Star Coins guide

In this guide, we’ll show you the locations of every Star Coin in New Super Mario Bros. U Deluxe’s Peach’s Castle world.

PEACH’S CASTLE 1 — METEOR MOAT

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: The first Star Coin is right in your path — just wait for the lava tide to subside, and avoid the meteors as you grab it.

FEATURED VIDEOS FROM POLYGON

Playing F1 on the ultimate PC racing rig

Playing F1 on the ultimate PC racing rig

Star Coin 2: After the checkpoint, you’ll see the second Star Coin encased in blocks. Ground pound through the first layer of bricks and cancel it (by pressing up on the thumbstick) before you go through the second layer and into the lava — all while avoiding meteors and the Bony Beetle! (It’s actually easiest to get under the bricks and let a meteor hit them — the meteor will fizzle on impact with the bricks, and you’ll have a moment to get clear before more meteors rain down.)

Star Coin 3: Toward the end of the level, where the lava tide is rising and falling, youll spot the last Star Coin in a gap between rocks. When the lava is at its highest point, jump down to collect it, then quickly wall jump out.

PEACH’S CASTLE 2 — MAGMA-RIVER CRUISE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: Early in the level, you will see the first Star Coin high up on a platform. To get there, jump on the nearby bricks, then jump up to find a hidden coin block. When you get on the block, the bricks below you will move up, giving you the final steppingstone you need to reach the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: You’ll see a structure of bricks with the second Star Coin in the middle. Don’t hit the nearby P Switch — it won’t bring down the Star Coin, but it will bring down dozens of Mini Goombas to swarm the boat and stop it in its tracks. Instead, jump up and break through the bricks the old-fashioned way to reach your Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: You’ll see two platforms and a red pipe marking the end of the level. Stay on the boat for just a bit longer — the final Star Coin is beyond the pipe opening.

PEACH’S CASTLE 3 — RISING TIDES OF LAVA

 GRID VIEW

1 of 5

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see this Star Coin near the beginning of the level. To reach it, drop down when the lava is low. As it begins to rise, jump on the giant Buzzy Beetles when they are floating. From here, jump up and reach the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: Underneath the checkpoint flag, you’ll see the second Star Coin. Backtrack to an opening under the platform, then wait for the lava tide to recede. As soon as it does, dash through the path (jumping whenever there’s a bump because running over them will just slow you down) and claim the Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin requires a bit of timing. Toward the end, you’ll spin on a screwtop mushroom platform to raise a second one out of the lava. Above this second platform, there are two question blocks with a space between them. You must raise the platform, jump to it, then jump up between the two question blocks. This will reveal a hidden block with a vine. Take the vine to a new area with the Star Coin. You can make some big jumps from the platforms to reach the Star Coin, but it’s easier to jump on the Fire Chomp in the area and boost yourself up to it.

PEACH’S CASTLE 4 — FIREFALL CLIFFS

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: When you see a POW Block, throw it to make the first Star Coin fall from above. Hurry and get it before the meteors destroy the blocks underneath it and it drops.

Star Coin 2: After the checkpoint, you’ll see rocks to climb on your left. Head up, and keep climbing after you see a signpost with a diagonal arrow — the Star Coin is above the rocks.

Star Coin 3: Near the end of the level, the path will split into three. The far right is a dead end, and the far left will take you to the exit. Heat up the middle path so you can collect the final Star Coin before finishing the level

PEACH’S CASTLE CASTLE — RED HOT ELEVATOR RIDE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 4

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: You’ll see the first Star Coin just below a ledge, above some Bowser Stunners. Don’t worry, you won’t be crushed against the ceiling! Swing on over and collect the Star Coin.

Star Coin 2: After a series of falling Bob-ombs, you’ll see a P Switch against the ceiling. Swing over and hit it — the trail of blue coins will point you to the second Star Coin.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin is near the top, among some Bowser Stunners. Time your movements to avoid the electrical shocks.

PEACH’S CASTLE CASTLE — THE FINAL BATTLE

 GRID VIEW

1 of 3

Nintendo EAD/Nintendo via Polygon

Star Coin 1: All the Star Coins in this short level are easy to see. The first one comes early on, in a gap between two platforms. Slide down the wall to collect it, then wall jump out.

Star Coin 2: You’ll see the second Star Coin floating atop a small platform, guarded by a row of bricks. The easiest way to get this is to stand on top of the bricks until Bowser Jr. takes aim at you, then jump out of the way and let him smash through the bricks for you. The Star Coin will then be yours for the taking.

Star Coin 3: The final Star Coin in the game appears just before the door leading to the checkpoint and the final battle. It’s hovering over some lava next to a wall. Slide down the wall to snag it, then wall jump to safety on the nearby platform.
